# near-moderated

Flagline `light` run, 2026-11-19. 797 text-bearing posts scored.

Part of the [Flagline ModerationBench results](README.md). See [metrics.md](metrics.md) for pooled numbers and the method.

## Metrics

| Precision | Recall | F1 | Accuracy | Flag rate | n |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0.7642 | 0.5704 | 0.6532 | 0.7842 | 26.6% | 797 |

Unsafe is the positive class. `flag` and `block` both count as Unsafe.

`Predicted` maps Flagline's `allow` to S0 and its top category to a Bluesky code. `Score` is the top category score on flagged rows and `1 - top score` on allowed rows. `Truth` shows the first annotated category when a post has several; [metrics.md](metrics.md) counts every annotated category.

## Confusion

|  | Actual Safe | Actual Unsafe |
|---|---|---|
| Predicted Safe | 463 | 122 |
| Predicted Unsafe | 50 | 162 |

## Decisions

| allow | flag | block | Top categories on flagged rows |
|---|---|---|---|
| 585 | 197 | 15 | harassment 105, sexual 46, violence 26, hate 19, self-harm 12, spam 4 |

## Media

| Text only | n | With media | n |
|---|---|---|---|
| 80.6% | 310 | 77.0% | 487 |

Media means the post had an image or video. Flagline did not see it.

## Mismatches (172)

False negatives: expected Unsafe, predicted Safe (122).
